## Corpus Check
- 1588 files · ~2,302,101 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 33673 nodes · 68537 edges · 1361 communities (1172 shown, 189 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 90% EXTRACTED · 10% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 6699 edges (avg confidence: 0.93)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `5cb9a3d0`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
